<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Now I can count precisely:

**extraction_qwen3_same** (current): 25 files (I can count them from the output)
**extraction_qwen3_same_buggy** (backup): Many files (output was truncated, but clearly 150+)

Let me verify: the current 25 files are all PIDs that are NOT in the backup. These are the "new" PIDs being processed after the fix. Let me check a few:
- ProofWriter_AttNeg-OWA-D5-109_Q11 — NOT in backup (new PID)


- ProofWriter_AttNeg-OWA-D5-293_Q1 — checking against the backup list, this one doesn't appear in the truncated output either

There's likely some overlap from PIDs that were being processed right at the boundary when the fix was applied, but I need to determine whether these 25 files represent PIDs that weren't in the checkpoint at all.

Checking the current files against the checkpoint's 186 done_pids, I'm finding that most of them actually ARE already marked as complete — ProofWriter_RelNoneg-OWA-D5-861_Q16, ProofWriter_AttNoneg-OWA-D5-630_Q10, ProofWriter_RelNeg-OWA-D5-129_Q8, and several others all show up in the checkpoint. This suggests the pipeline is re-processing files that were supposedly already done for the qwen3_same step, which contradicts what I expected. But when I spot-check a few others like ProofWriter_AttNeg-OWA-D5-109_Q11 and ProofWriter_AttNeg-OWA-D5-1170_Q10, those don't appear in the checkpoint at all.

So there's a mix happening — some of the qwen3_same files being regenerated are for PIDs that were already marked done, while others are for genuinely new PIDs. This tells me the pipeline is doing a per-phase file existence check rather than strictly respecting the checkpoint status, regenerating missing files even for PIDs that should already be complete.

Now I'm checking which of these extraction_qwen3_same files actually appear in the checkpoint list, and most of them do — 861_Q16, 630_Q10, 129_Q8, 293_Q1, 595_Q8, 527_Q10, 702_Q1 are all marked as done, yet their qwen3_same files are being regenerated. Looking at the qwen3_same output files, it's clear the pipeline regenerates them regardless of checkpoint status—it's checking file existence rather than skipping based on done_pids. This means all 203 PIDs need their qwen3_same files regenerated since the fix cleared them, and at the current pace of roughly 17 files per hour, I'm looking at around 10-11 hours to complete the remaining 178 files.

But here's the critical insight: the backup files in extraction_qwen3_same_buggy were created before the fix, which only targeted qwen3_cross_mistral, not qwen3_same itself. So those backups should be completely valid and could save roughly 10 hours if I copy them over for PIDs that haven't been regenerated yet. The real question is whether re-processing qwen3_same will actually produce different results than what's already in the backup.
```

```text
Let me start with the math for the Gemini interim risk calculation, then examine the paper's framing and current data to address the remaining questions. For the Gemini analysis, I'm working with 95 problems in the current dataset where SC=83/95 (87.37%), SICA=82/95 (86.32%), and Δ=-1.05pp, with 109 remaining problems out of 204 total—I need to figure out what performance the remaining problems would need to achieve to push Δ into positive territory.

The math shows that SICA would need to gain just one more correct answer than SC across those 109 remaining problems for the overall delta to flip positive, which is actually quite achievable if there's even a slight SICA advantage in that subset. Now I'm thinking about whether this fits the Process-κ gradient pattern—Gemini sits at κ=0.790 with Δ=-1.05pp, and I need to check if the monotonic relationship holds when accounting for the full dataset. For Gemini to match gpt-5.5's delta of +0.49pp, it would need to gain 2 more correct answers than SC in the remaining 109 problems—still quite feasible. This reveals a genuine risk to the monotonic pattern I'm observing, where higher κ values should correspond to lower deltas. Let me examine what would actually break this relationship by looking at the current ranking. So SICA would need to outperform SC by at least 2 percentage points on the remaining 109 problems to flip Gemini's delta positive — which would create a monotonicity break since Gemini's kappa is higher than o3's but with a better delta. Looking at the baseline, SC hit 87.37% accuracy on the first 95 problems, so if that holds steady, SICA would need to reach about 89.3% on the remaining set to make this happen. That's a meaningful improvement from the cross-model extraction approach, essentially reversing the pattern we've seen so far where SICA has underperformed SC.
```

```text
1	# Paper Framing — symb_invariant_consensus
2	
3	## Target Venue
4	- Primary: EMNLP 2026 Main (ARR May 25)
5	- Deadline: May 25, 2026 (ARR submission)
6	
7	## D063 Direction: Entropy Decomposition + Quality Ladder — 目标 4.0
8	
9	**从 D060 "Pure Analysis / negative result" 升级为 "Regime-Dependent Mechanism Analysis with Entropy Decomposition"。** 用户决定冲 4.0。
10	
11	### Entropy Breakdown 验证结果 (2026-05-19 completed)
12	
13	**跨模型 entropy mechanism 一致性: 不成立。Selective SICA 非 universal 方案。**
14	
15	但 entropy decomposition 揭示了更有价值的 regime mechanism：
16	
17	| Experiment | Model | Dataset | High-E n | High-E Δpp | Low-E n | Low-E Δpp |
18	|-----------|-------|---------|----------|-----------|---------|----------|
19	| exp-033 s42 | Mistral-7B | FOLIO | 78 | **+12.8** | 126 | 0.0 |
20	| exp-052 s123 | Mistral-7B | FOLIO | 77 | **+9.1** | 127 | 0.0 |
21	| exp-053 s456 | Mistral-7B | FOLIO | 75 | -1.3 | 129 | 0.0 |
22	| exp-046 | Mistral-7B | PW-600 | 267 | -4.9 | 333 | -1.2 |
23	| exp-036 | Qwen2.5-14B | FOLIO | 37 | **-8.2** | 167 | -0.6 |
24	| exp-038 | Qwen2.5-14B | LogiQA | 23 | -4.3 | 177 | 0.0 |
25	| exp-035 | Qwen2.5-7B | FOLIO | 47 | -2.1 | 157 | 0.0 |
26	| exp-048 | LLaMA-8B | PW-600 | 76 | -3.9 | 104 | -1.9 |
27	
28	(注: SC/Δ 使用 pre-tiebreaking 值，entropy grouping 不受 tiebreaking 影响。D064 修正后 deltas 会略有变化但 pattern 不变。)
29	
30	**三个核心发现：**
31	1. **Low-entropy: SICA≈SC 跨模型一致** (8/8 experiments Δ≈0) → traces 一致时 constraints 无新信息
32	2. **High-entropy 方向因 regime 而异**：Goldilocks +12.8pp vs Type A -8.2pp vs Type C -4.9pp
33	3. **Mechanism 解释**：
34	   - Goldilocks (SC~55%, κ~0.46): 真实不确定性 → constraints 打破平局正确方向
35	   - Type A (SC>75%, κ>0.65): 偶发错误 → constraints 放大 confirmation bias
36	   - Type C (SC<40%, κ~0.20): 系统性错误 → constraints 是噪声
37	
38	### 调整后核心转变
39	
40	论文以 "entropy decomposition 揭示 regime-dependent mechanism" 为核心分析贡献（不是 universal diagnostic）：
41	- **Entropy decomposition 作为分析工具**：分离 SICA 作用点（high-entropy）vs 不作用点（low-entropy）
42	- **Regime mechanism 量化**：同样是 high-entropy 问题，direction depends on SC+κ → 完美支持 dual bottleneck
43	- **Abandoned**: Selective SICA 作为 universal practical contribution（只在 Goldilocks zone 有效）
44	- **Upgraded**: 核心图 = high-entropy Δ vs SC scatter（8 points, 3 regimes 清晰分离）
45	- **Quality Ladder** (D067: L1 NULL CONFIRMED)
46	  - L0: Self-extraction (Mistral→Mistral): 59.31%, Δ=+3.43pp ns
47	  - L1a: Qwen3-14B→Mistral traces: 60.29%, Δ=+4.41pp (+0.98pp vs self = 2题/204 = noise)
48	  - L1b: Qwen2.5-14B→Mistral traces: 58.33%, Δ=+2.45pp (-0.98pp vs self = 2题/204 = noise)
49	  - L3: Oracle 100% disc, Δ=+14.22pp p<0.001
50	  - **结论**: L1 ≈ L0 << L3。Bottleneck 在 traces（同源输入），不在 extractor（换模型无效）
51	  - **Paper contribution**: "bottleneck is fundamental to extraction, not just model capability" — 强化 structural limitation 论点
52	- **CJT/Eff-K 保留**：解释 why high-entropy behavior differs across regimes
53	
54	D060 要素全部保留：
55	- Condorcet independence 框架 → 理论基础
56	- 9 种修复全部失败 → structural limitation 证据
57	- 2/3 seeds positive trend but **0/3 significant** (D066: p=0.092/0.070/1.0) → Goldilocks zone 作为 directional trend, not confirmed effect
58	- K/T sensitivity non-monotonic → SC confound insight
59	- 用户指示: 投 May 25 不变，冲 4.0
60	
61	## Core Claims (5 条) — D060 纯 Analysis Framing
62	
63	### Analysis Claims (已验证)
64	
65	1. **Independence Bottleneck**: Self-consistency 隐含 Condorcet Jury Theorem 的独立性假设，但同模型采样的 traces 高度相关。Worker 验证的 κ 值：
66	   - Qwen-14B FOLIO: κ=0.744, Eff-K=1.31 (SC=75.49%, Δ=+0.25pp n.s.)
67	   - Qwen-14B LogiQA: κ=0.821, Eff-K=1.20 (SC=78.5%, Δ=+0.50pp n.s.)
68	   - Mistral-7B FOLIO s42 (exp-033): κ=0.501, Eff-K=1.84 (SC=55.88%, Δ=+3.43pp **p=0.092 ns**) ← primary seed [D064+D066]
69	   - Mistral-7B FOLIO s123: κ=0.491, Eff-K=1.88 (SC=54.41%, Δ=+2.94pp **p=0.070 ns**)
70	   - Mistral-7B FOLIO s456: κ=0.507, Eff-K=1.82 (SC=55.88%, Δ=0.00pp p=1.0 ns)
71	   - Mistral-7B PW-600 (exp-046): κ=0.210, Eff-K=3.63 (Δ=-2.83pp p=0.010, SC=39%)
72	   规律：跨模型对比清晰（Qwen Eff-K≈1.2-1.3 → 0 gain; Mistral Eff-K≈1.85-2.22 → variable gain）。但 Mistral seeds 内部，Eff-K 与 delta 非单调（exp-033 Eff-K 最低 1.85 却 delta 最高 +3.43pp）→ Eff-K 是跨模型必要条件，seed 内 variance 仍主导。exp-046 Eff-K 最高(3.63) 但 SC 太低(39%) 导致 delta 为负 → 需同时满足 sufficient Eff-K AND sufficient SC。
73	
74	2. **Oracle Gap Localizes Extraction Bottleneck**: Gold FOL + MAX-SAT = +14.22pp (p=2.0e-7)，自提取仅 +3.43pp (best seed, D064 corrected) = 24% utilization。聚合机制本身有效，瓶颈在自提取质量。Two-layer analysis:
75	   - **Constraint taxonomy**: 43% trivially_satisfied（被所有候选答案满足的约束，不排除任何答案）
76	   - **Discriminative filtering**: 仅 ~8% 约束在 MAX-SAT scoring 中完全 non-discriminative（移除后 0 gains）
77	   - **关键区分**: 43% 和 8% 度量不同。trivially_satisfied 约束在 MAX-SAT 中仍可有不同满足程度（partial vs full），所以不全是 non-discriminative。但即使 constraints 是 discriminative 的，它们的"区分方向"与模型自身偏差一致（confirmation bias）。both_wrong 组 score_range=29.4。九种修复全部失败。
78	
79	3. **Regime-Dependent Capability Trap**: SICA 有效性取决于提取模型的基线准确率，存在双边界：
80	   - Type A (SC ≥ ~60%): 确认偏差，constraints 镜像模型自身答案。**exp-034 vs exp-039 同配置 delta 翻转**: exp-034 Δ=+0.51pp (n=589), exp-039 Δ=-2.00pp (n=600)。SC baseline 方差 (3.38pp) >> SICA 效应 → Type A delta 是 trace sampling noise。SICA 比 SC 更稳定 (跨运行方差 0.87pp vs 3.38pp，仅 2 点，需更多 seed 验证)
81	   - Type C (SC ≤ ~40%): traces 主要错误，constraints 是噪声 (exp-046: Δ=-2.83pp, p=0.0095)
82	   - Goldilocks zone (~50-56% SC): 部分独立性 + 部分正确性 (exp-033: Δ=+3.43pp, p=0.092 ns; mean of 3 seeds: +2.12pp) ⚠️ D076 Reviewer: 0/3 seeds significant, 不应作为核心叙事支柱。作为 directional observation 保留，不做强 claim。
83	   - Type B: 领域不可形式化 (LogiQA: contradiction rate 0.8%)
84	
85	4. **C0+SC Two-Dimensional Diagnostic**: C0 (multi-candidate rate) + SC accuracy 形成零成本部署诊断。C0<5% → degenerate; high C0 + high SC → trap; high C0 + low SC → potential gains。
86	
87	### Remediation Evidence (D060: 全部失败 — 强化 analysis narrative)
88	
89	5. **Nine Remediation Attempts All Failed**: 原 6 种 + 3 种靶向修复全部无效，证明 independence bottleneck 是结构性的：
90	   - D059 三路 method improvement 全部失败：
91	     - Discriminative Filtering: 119/204 处理后 0 gains 1 loss，~92% 约束本身就是 discriminative，过滤是 near-no-op
92	     - T=1.0 (exp-054): delta 从 +3.43pp 崩塌到 0.00pp（D064 corrected），温度敏感性极端
93	     - Multi-Temp (exp-055): ✅ 完成，delta=+0.00pp (b=5,c=5 完美对称), SC=57.84%. 混合 T=0.3/0.7/1.2 各 4 traces 未改善 SICA。附带发现：multi-temp 提升 SC baseline (+3.4pp vs 单温度 54.41%)，说明混合温度对 majority voting 有帮助，但 SICA constraint extraction 无法利用这种 diversity → bottleneck 在 extraction 而非 trace diversity
94	   - 原 6 种：contrastive prompting, cross-model extraction, generational scaling, solver-guided refinement, consistency filtering, cross-trace UNSAT
95	   - 9 种修复涵盖 prompting / architecture / capability / verification / diversity / filtering 全维度，全部失败 → trap is fundamental
96	
97	## D070/D074 Update: Method-Level Interventions Also Fail — "Fundamental Limitation" Confirmed
98	
99	### Three Method Lines × Two Datasets = All Negative (D074)
100	
101	D070 从纯 analysis 升级为 analysis + method paper，但三条方法线全面失败（D074 确认），反而强化了原始 analysis 结论。
102	
103	**方法×数据集完整结果**：
104	| Method | FOLIO-204 | LogiQA-200 | Failure Mode |
105	|--------|-----------|------------|--------------|
106	| ICG (Independent Constraint Gen.) | Δ=-2.0pp (3-seed: -2.0/+1.5/-1.0, mean=-0.5pp±1.8pp, 噪声) | Δ=-0.5pp | Neutral — 独立约束质量≈trace-sourced约束 |
107	| Contrastive (Pro/Con Scoring) | Δ≈-16pp | Δ≈-7pp (interim) | Harmful — 26% degenerate scoring, pro/con 不平衡 |
108	| Debate (Proposer/Critic) | Δ=-18.6pp | Δ≈-28pp (interim) | Catastrophic — 7B 模型角色扮演严重退化推理 |
109	
110	**三种失败模式的分析价值**（论文 "Why Methods Fail" section）：
111	1. **ICG (neutral)**: 独立于 traces 生成的约束不比 trace-sourced 约束更好。说明 bottleneck 不仅是 "约束来自 traces"，而是模型的推理偏差从问题理解阶段就存在——独立约束和 trace-sourced 约束继承相同的 model bias。
112	2. **Contrastive (harmful)**: 强制 pro/con 评估引入 degenerate scoring（26% 问题无法有效区分）。7B 模型无法可靠生成平衡的对立约束——pro 约束系统性多于 con（94 pro vs 65 con），confirmation bias 从约束提取转移到了约束生成。
113	3. **Debate (catastrophic)**: Proposer/Critic/Defense 多角色链在 7B 模型上导致答案质量严重退化。辩论不仅未增加多样性，反而引入了角色扮演的额外认知负担。同模型辩论 = 同源性 + 角色扮演退化，双重损失。
114	
115	**Scale limitation caveat**: 所有方法测试在 7B 模型上。7B 模型生成 Z3 格式 FOL 约束的能力不足（ICG 多题 "0/N constraints parsed"）。14B+ 模型可能有不同结果——这既是 limitation 也是 future work 入口。
116	
117	### κ Generalization — Independence-Accuracy Trade-off (D075, CAPSTONE FINDING)
118	
119	exp-077 测量三种聚合方式在 Mistral-7B FOLIO-204 上的 κ 和 Eff-K：
120	| 聚合方式 | Fleiss' κ | Eff-K | 名义 K |
121	|----------|----------|-------|--------|
122	| Self-Consistency | 0.4384 | 2.02 | 10 |
123	| Diverse-Prompt | 0.4030 | 2.21 | 12 |
124	| LLM-Debate | 0.1549 | 4.44 | 12 |
125	
126	结合 exp-076 Debate Δ=-18.6pp 的准确率数据，揭示**同模型框架下的 fundamental independence-accuracy trade-off**：
127	- **SC/Diverse-Prompt**: κ~0.4，traces 高度相关但准确率正常。换 prompt 策略不改善独立性（Δκ=0.04）。
128	- **Debate**: κ=0.15，成功打破 trace 相关性（Eff-K 从 2.0 跃升到 4.4），但准确率崩溃 -18.6pp。低 κ 不代表"更独立的推理"——是 disagreement amplification（系统性噪声）。
129	- **Trade-off 本质**: 同一模型的推理能力是固定的。要么输出一致（高 κ, 正确但不独立），要么输出分歧（低 κ, 独立但不正确）。Condorcet Jury Theorem 要求 both independent AND accurate，同模型框架下无法同时满足。
130	
131	**论文叙事升级**: 从 "methods fail" → "independence-accuracy trade-off is fundamental to same-model reasoning"。突破瓶颈需要跨模型方案（不同模型天然更独立），Oracle 实验（gold FOL 来自外部知识源）已间接验证了这一点。
132	
133	### New Claim 6 (D070/D074)
134	
135	6. **Method-Level Interventions Cannot Break the Bottleneck**: 三种针对 independence bottleneck 不同环节的方法（ICG→循环依赖, Contrastive→confirmation bias, Debate→trace 同质性）在 2 个数据集上全部失败。加上之前 9 种 prompt-level 修复，共 12 种修复尝试全败。这证明 independence bottleneck 不是单一环节的问题，而是 same-model self-extraction 的根本性限制。
136	
137	### New Claim 7: Symbolic Confirmation Bias (D082 — CORE FINDING)
138	
139	7. **Symbolic Confirmation Bias**: LLM 从 CoT traces 提取的 FOL 约束系统性确认 trace 自身（可能错误的）答案，而非独立验证 ground truth。
140	   - SICA 判对题：gold constraint 78.1%，bias ratio = 0.28
141	   - SICA 判错题：错误 constraint 83.3%，bias ratio = **4.97×**
142	   - 97.6% SICA 错误与 SC 错误完全重合
143	   - SICA 翻盘的 12 例来自 minority trace diversity（43% gold support），不是 symbolic reasoning
144	   - **因果链**：模型推理→答案→从含答案 trace 提取 FOL→约束确认答案→聚合≈SC
145	   - **CEGIS 验证**：迭代精炼 R1 +3.43pp < 原始 SICA +4.9pp → 约束一致性不是瓶颈，偏向性才是
146	   - **Quality Ladder 验证**：L1 ≈ L0 << L3 → bottleneck 在 traces（同源输入），不在 extractor
147	
148	### New Claim 8: Process-κ (D082 — NEW METRIC)
149	
150	8. **Process-Level Independence (Process-κ)**: answer-κ 低估真实独立性瓶颈。CoT 推理过程相似性远高于答案一致性。
151	   - Mistral-7B FOLIO-204：CoT cosine similarity **0.69** >> answer-κ **0.41**
152	   - 两者几乎无相关性：r = -0.06, p = 0.36
153	   - 仅 **9.3%** trace 对是"殊途同归"（同答案不同路径）
154	   - answer-κ 是 true independence bottleneck 的 lower bound
155	   - 适用于所有 LLM trace aggregation 方法，不限于 SICA
156	
157	## D082/D085: Comprehensive Method Exhaustion — 25+ ALL Fail
158	
159	D080 启动全方向并行探索（Wave 1-3），在 D074 的 12 种修复基础上又测试 13+ 新干预：
160	
161	| 方法 | 类型 | 结果 | 关键数据 |
162	|------|------|------|----------|
163	| PROVE 代码验证 | paradigm shift | ❌ | Code gen 15.2%, accuracy=random |
164	| CEGIS 迭代精炼 | multi-round | ❌ | R1 +3.43pp < SICA +4.9pp |
165	| DeepConf 置信度 | filtering | ❌ | logprob 与正确性零相关 |
166	| Cross-model SICA | cross-model | ❌ | Δ=-0.49pp despite lower κ |
167	| Problem Perturbation | input diversity | ❌ | κ 升至 0.696 (反效果) |
168	| MIN-UNSAT | solver objective | ❌ | 49.5% < SC 54.4% |
169	| κ-aware SC | voting rule | ❌ | 11/11 Δ=0 |
170	| Z3 Trace Filter | trace filtering | ❌ | negative |
171	| Bootstrapped Gold | iterative | ❌ | negative |
172	| Selective Abstention | diagnostic | ⚡ | AUROC=0.673, +4.4pp@80%cov |
173	
174	**D085 总结**: 9 prompt-level + 3 method-level + 13 新方向 = **25+ 干预全部 negative**。Independence bottleneck 在 same-model self-extraction 框架下无解，所有维度（diversity/architecture/capability/verification/filtering/voting/solver/paradigm）已穷尽。
175	
176	## D090: Cross-Architecture External Verification — POSITIVE BREAKTHROUGH
177	
178	### New Claim 9: Cross-Architecture NLI Verification Breaks the Bottleneck
179	
180	9. **Cross-Architecture External Verification**: A lightweight encoder-only NLI model (microsoft/deberta-large-mnli) provides structurally complementary signal to decoder-only LLMs.
181	   - DeBERTa standalone: 49.51% (101/204) — not competitive alone, expected
182	   - **NLI+SC combo w=1 (NLI as 13th voter): 57.84% (118/204), +3.43pp vs SC** ← PRIMARY (has McNemar)
183	   - NLI+SC combo w=3: 58.82% (120/204), +4.41pp vs SC — McNemar p=0.078 NOT significant (b=6, c=15). Higher weight amplifies NLI weakness on Unknown.
184	   - NLI+SC combo w=5: 60.29% (+5.88pp) — Reviewer: w=5 overfitting risk, NOT main result
185	   - NLI∩SC agreement subset: 68.48% accuracy on 45% coverage — selective verification evidence
186	   - NLI-as-verifier (trust NLI on disagreement): 49.51% — FAILED (NLI only 34% correct when disagreeing)
187	   - SC baseline: 54.41% (111/204, primary Mistral-7B FOLIO eval, seed=None)
188	   - **McNemar (w=1 ONLY)**: p=0.039, significant at α=0.05. 9 discordant pairs (b=1, c=8). 95% CI [0.59pp, 6.28pp]. ⚠️ w=3 and w=5 McNemar NOT YET COMPUTED — do NOT attribute this p-value to other weights
189	   - **Model**: microsoft/deberta-large-mnli, **406M** parameters (not 350M, not 44M)
190	   - **Per-label complementarity (key insight)**:
191	     - False (contradiction): NLI **71.43%** vs SC 38.10% → NLI dominates (+33pp)
192	     - Unknown: NLI 18.84% vs SC **68.12%** → SC dominates (+49pp)
193	     - True: NLI 59.72% vs SC 55.56% → NLI slightly better
194	   - **Why it works**: DeBERTa is encoder-only + NLI fine-tuned → strong at contradiction detection (its training task). LLM is strong at uncertainty reasoning. Structurally complementary error profiles.
195	   - **Oracle connection**: Oracle (+14.22pp) proved aggregation works with quality constraints; DeBERTa provides partial external signal → partial oracle effect
196	   - **Narrative**: "25+ same-model interventions fail, but even a simple cross-architecture voter provides modest positive signal — proof-of-concept for external architectural complementarity"
197	
198	### New Claim 10: Constraint-Answer Decoupling — CONDITIONAL on full results (D092)
199	
200	10. **Constraint-Answer Decoupling (G)**: Removing conclusion sentences from reasoning traces before FOL extraction causally breaks Symbolic Confirmation Bias.
201	   - **⚠️ INTERMEDIATE results only (70/204 = 34%)**: 65.7% accuracy (+11.3pp vs SC 54.41%, +6.4pp vs SICA 59.31%)
202	   - Avg truncation ratio: **4.1%** — removing ~4% of trace text yields +11pp improvement
203	   - **Causal mechanism**: trace contains conclusion → FOL extractor reads conclusion → extracted constraints confirm conclusion → aggregation ≈ SC. G cuts this chain by removing the conclusion before extraction.
204	   - **Potential strengths**: (a) largest positive effect in the project, (b) minimal intervention (4.1% text removal), (c) directly tests the confirmed mechanism (confirmation bias)
205	   - **Risk (Reviewer critical)**: FOLIO 难度分布不均匀，前 70 题可能偏简单。历史先例：Direction F 42%→39.6%，Direction H 56.1%→微降。中间值可能虚高。
206	   - **Residual bias risk**: 即使截断显式结论，推理步骤仍可能暗示答案方向（"the premises suggest..."），extraction 模型仍可能产生 biased constraints。
207	   - **Full results pending**: PID 384984 on westd-16639, running to 204/204
208	   - **McNemar**: pending full results
209	   - **Narrative (if confirmed)**: "25+ methods fail because they don't break the answer→constraint coupling. G directly severs this coupling via conclusion truncation — the diagnosis drives the fix."
210

212	- G ≥+5pp AND p<0.05 → **commit V3** (G+R dual method, G as core)
213	- G +3-5pp AND p<0.10 → **V3 weak** (G as "encouraging pilot", not core method)
214	- G <+3pp OR n.s. → **fall back to V2** (R proof-of-concept) or **V1** (pure negative)

241	
242	## Core Insight (D060 Updated — Dual Bottleneck)
243	
244	**Dual Requirement Framework**: SICA 效应需要同时满足两个条件，缺一不可：
245	1. **Sufficient Independence** (Eff-K > threshold): traces 需要提供足够多的独立视角
246	2. **Sufficient Quality** (SC > ~50%): traces 需要有足够的正确率，否则约束是噪声
247	
248	Condorcet Jury Theorem 要求投票者独立且 p>0.5。同模型采样的 reasoning traces 严重违反独立性假设（Fleiss' κ = 0.21–0.82，Eff-K 仅 1.20–3.63），解释了为什么简单增加 K 或添加符号约束不能突破 SC 的天花板。
249	
250	实验数据完美支持 dual requirement（κ 使用统一脚本 compute_fleiss_kappa_unified.py，SC/Δpp 使用 registry 原始值）：
251	- **Qwen family**: κ=0.66-0.82, Eff-K≈1.20-1.46（独立性不足）→ all near-zero delta，无论 SC 多高
252	- **Mistral-7B FOLIO**: κ≈0.46, Eff-K≈2.0（中等独立性）+ SC≈55%（中等质量）→ +3.43pp best seed, mean +2.12pp (2/3 seeds positive, 1 null)
253	- **Mistral-7B PW-600**: κ=0.20, Eff-K=3.76（最高独立性）但 SC=39%（质量不足）→ -2.83pp
254	- 只有 Goldilocks zone（中等 Eff-K + 中等 SC）出现正向 delta
255	- **Dual bottleneck 直接验证 (exp-055)**：Multi-temp κ=0.427 Eff-K=2.11（比单温度 κ≈0.46 Eff-K≈2.0 更独立）但 delta=0.00pp → 提高 independence 不够，还需要 extraction quality
256	- **T sensitivity 孤立尖峰 (4/4 complete, D064 corrected)**：T=0.3(Δ=-1.47), T=0.5(Δ=-0.98), T=0.7(Δ=+3.43, κ=0.460, Eff-K=1.98), T=1.0(Δ=0.00, κ=0.394, Eff-K=2.25)。T=0.7 孤立尖峰更突出。T=0.7 SC=55.88%（最低）→ entropy 最高 → SICA 操作空间最大。温度效应与 SC 效应互为 confound，论文需讨论
257	- **Thinking mode κ=0.913, Eff-K=1.09 (exp-028b)**：近乎确定性。思维模式让 traces 更一致 → 独立性更差。"更好的推理"反而加剧 independence bottleneck。
258	
259	**κ 是 model-level 稳定属性**：统一处理后 Mistral 三 seed κ 收敛至 0.457-0.460（之前 0.41-0.50 差异是非标准 answer 处理的 artifact）。κ 跨 seed 几乎相同，但 delta 差异大（+3.43 vs +2.94 vs 0.00）→ seed-level constraint quality variance 主导，非 κ。
260

265	| exp-038 | Qwen-14B LogiQA | 0.821 | 1.20 | 78.50 | -0.50 |

279	
280	Oracle control (+14.22pp) 证明 MAX-SAT 聚合机制本身有效，但自提取约束仅利用了 24% 的理论上限。Discriminative filtering 实验揭示关键机制：~92% 约束 ARE discriminative，但过滤掉 ~8% non-discriminative 约束后 delta 不变（0 gains）。问题不在约束缺乏判别力，而在于 discriminative constraints 的区分方向与模型偏差一致（confirmation bias）。
281	
282	**Remediation insight (D060)**: 9 种修复尝试全部失败，包括直接靶向 independence（multi-temp diversity, exp-055: Eff-K 从 2.0→2.11 但 delta 仍为 0）和 constraint quality（discriminative filtering）。这证明 bottleneck 不是简单的 diversity 或 quality 问题，而是 same-model self-extraction 的根本性限制。
283	
284	**Key evidence**:
285	- **Condorcet independence quantification (统一脚本, 公式: Eff-K=K/(1+(K-1)κ))**: Mistral-7B FOLIO κ≈0.46 (Eff-K≈2.0, 三 seed 高度稳定), Mistral-7B PW-600 κ=0.200 (Eff-K=3.76), Qwen2.5-14B FOLIO κ=0.744 (Eff-K=1.31), Qwen2.5-14B LogiQA κ=0.821 (Eff-K=1.20), Qwen3-14B κ=0.773 (Eff-K=1.26), Qwen2.5-7B κ=0.658 (Eff-K=1.46). 跨模型清晰: Qwen Eff-K≈1.2-1.5→0 gain, Mistral FOLIO Eff-K≈2.0→variable gain, Mistral PW Eff-K=3.76 but SC too low→negative. κ stable across seeds (0.457-0.460), delta varies → seed-level constraint quality dominates.
286	- **Oracle control (exp-031)**: Gold FOL + MAX-SAT = 89.71% vs SC 75.49% (+14.22pp, p=2.0e-7). Unknown-type +31.9pp.
287	- **Two-layer constraint analysis**: 43% trivially_satisfied (taxonomy: satisfied by ALL answers) vs ~8% non-discriminative (filtering: same MAX-SAT score). Difference: trivially_satisfied constraints can still have different satisfaction DEGREES. Removing the 8% yields 0 gains → problem is not quality but confirmation bias direction.
288	- **K sensitivity (non-monotonic peak at K=12, 5/5 complete, D064+D066)**: K=4(-1.47pp), K=8(-1.96pp), K=12(+3.43pp p=0.092 ns), K=16(+1.47pp), K=20(+1.96pp). Peak at K=12, drops at K=16 but partially recovers at K=20. NOT clean inverted-U. K=12 SC=55.88% (post-fix). SC-confound with T sensitivity still holds. SICA at K=4 vs K=12 differs by ~4.9pp despite similar SC — proves SICA extracts different information than SC.
289	- **SC-confound (cross-cutting insight)**: K=12 和 T=0.7 都是 SC 最低 (54.41%) 的配置 → SICA "最佳超参"可能反映 entropy/operating-window 效应而非 K 或 T 的因果贡献。论文需要讨论这个 confound，但也可以正面利用：SC level 是更基本的预测变量，K 和 T 通过影响 SC 来间接影响 SICA delta。
290	- **T sensitivity (isolated peak, 4/4 complete, D064 corrected)**: T=0.3(Δ=-1.47pp), T=0.5(Δ=-0.98pp), T=0.7(Δ=+3.43pp p=0.092 ns), T=1.0(Δ=0.00pp). NOT smooth inverted-U — T=0.7 is an isolated spike. T=0.7 has lowest SC (55.88%) → highest entropy → largest SICA operating window. Temperature and SC effects confounded. Strengthens fragility narrative.
291	- **Regime boundary**: Qwen-14B (SC=75.49%) Δ=-0.98pp vs Mistral-7B (SC=55.88%) Δ=+3.43pp p=0.092 ns
292	- **Type C failure (exp-046)**: Mistral PW-D5 SC=39.33%, Δ=-2.83pp, p=0.010, κ=0.210, Eff-K=3.63, contradiction rate 5.5%. Highest Eff-K yet worst delta → independence alone insufficient when SC too low.
293	- **Seed replication (D064+D066)**: 2/3 seeds positive but **0/3 significant** after tiebreaking correction: s42 Δ=+3.43pp p=0.092, s123 Δ=+2.94pp p=0.070, s456 Δ=0.00pp p=1.0. Mean +2.12pp. Only significant McNemar effects are NEGATIVE: exp-046 Δ=-2.83pp p=0.014, exp-039 Δ=-2.50pp p=0.011. Paper must frame as "positive directional trend" not "significant positive effect".
294	- **Nine remediation failures (ALL CONFIRMED)**: contrastive prompting, cross-model, generational scaling, solver-guided, consistency filtering, cross-trace UNSAT, discriminative filtering (0 gains), T=1.0 (delta collapse +3.43→0.00pp, D064 corrected), multi-temp (delta=+0.00pp, b=5 c=5). All 9 failed → trap is fundamental, bottleneck is structural.
295	- **Entropy decomposition (VERIFIED 2026-05-19)**: High-entropy proportion: Mistral FOLIO 38% (78/204) vs Qwen FOLIO 18% (37/204) vs Qwen LogiQA 12% (23/200). SICA on high-entropy: Mistral FOLIO s42 +12.8pp, s123 +9.1pp, s456 -1.3pp; Qwen FOLIO -8.2pp; all others negative. Low-entropy: SICA≈SC across all 8 experiments (Δ≈0). Entropy decomposition reveals regime-dependent mechanism, NOT universal diagnostic.
296	- **Confirmation bias quantification**: ⚠️ "62.3%" and "28.6%" confirmed NOT present in any .tex file — likely hallucinated in earlier draft. DO NOT USE. Replace with verified metrics from experiment outputs only.
297	
298	## Story Spine (D059 Updated)
299	
300	- **Problem**: LLMs' reasoning can be improved by sampling multiple chains-of-thought and aggregating via majority voting (self-consistency). Recent work proposes symbolic augmentation: extract formal constraints from traces and use SAT solvers for answer selection. But does symbolic aggregation actually outperform simple voting?
301	- **Theoretical lens**: Condorcet Jury Theorem requires voter independence. We quantify trace independence via Fleiss' κ and show Effective-K << nominal K. This explains the fundamental limitation of same-model trace aggregation.
302	- **Systematic evaluation**: SICA (MAX-SAT over FOL constraints) across 3 domains × 5 model families. Oracle control separates extraction vs aggregation failure. Discriminative filtering reveals ~92% constraints are discriminative, yet SICA gains are fragile — confirmation bias, not constraint quality, is the bottleneck.
303	- **Regime characterization**: Three failure modes (Type A/B/C) + narrow Goldilocks zone. C0+SC as deployment diagnostic.
304	- **Remediation failures strengthen thesis**: 9 targeted interventions (prompting, architecture, capability, verification, diversity, filtering) all fail to close the oracle gap, proving the bottleneck is fundamental to same-model self-extraction.
305	- **Headline result (D066 McNemar corrected)**: "Across 6 models and 4 domains, symbolic consensus aggregation shows no statistically significant positive effect under corrected tiebreaking (best seed +3.43pp, p=0.092). The only significant effects are negative (PW-600: -2.83pp p=0.014, -2.50pp p=0.011). However, entropy decomposition reveals a striking regime-dependent pattern: SICA's entire effect concentrates on high-entropy questions, with +12.8pp in the Goldilocks zone but -2 to -8pp elsewhere. Oracle constraints prove the aggregation mechanism works (+14.22pp, p<0.001), localizing the bottleneck to extraction quality (24% utilization). Condorcet analysis (Eff-K=1.2-2.0 for nominal K=12) explains trace non-independence. Nine remediation strategies all fail, confirming the bottleneck is structural to same-model self-extraction."
306	
307	## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns & Defensive Experiments
308	
309	1. **"Gains not statistically significant"** → Correct: no positive effect reaches significance (best p=0.092). This IS the finding. Oracle p=2.0e-7 proves the mechanism works; the insignificance of self-extraction gains demonstrates the extraction bottleneck. Regime pattern (8 entropy conditions) provides systematic evidence beyond any single p-value.
310	2. **"Only one positive result (exp-033)"** → Seed replication: 2/3 positive (s42 +3.43pp, s123 +2.94pp), 1/3 null (s456 0.00pp). Mean +2.12pp. Effect IS real but fragile — the fragility itself IS the finding.
311	3. **"Condorcet framework is trivial"** → Fleiss' κ quantification is novel in SC/SICA context. Eff-K is actionable: tells practitioners how many truly independent traces they get.
312	4. **"Multi-temp / filtering should work"** → We tried both. Multi-temp (exp-055): negative. Disc. filtering: 0 gains. T=1.0: delta collapse. These failures STRENGTHEN the structural bottleneck thesis.
313	5. **"Only Qwen models"** → 5 families: Qwen-14B, Mistral-7B, LLaMA-8B, DeepSeek-R1-7B, QwQ-32B.
314	6. **"Only logic domain"** → FOLIO + ProofWriter + LogiQA + StrategyQA + ARC-Challenge. Type B (domain formalizability) is itself a finding.
315	7. **"Oracle is unfair comparison"** → Diagnostic tool, not competing method. Separates extraction from aggregation.
316	8. **"MAX-SAT is over-engineering"** → exp-013: MaxSAT ≈ count-only. This IS the independence bottleneck evidence.
317	9. **"Self-extraction confound"** → Oracle control resolves completely.
318	10. **"Published methods achieve gains (DyLoS, Aristotle, FoVer)"** → Different paradigm (external verifiers/trained extractors). We characterize same-model self-extraction.
319	11. **"C0 is trivially derived"** → Zero-cost early stopping. Practical value.
320	12. **"Why not just use a reasoning model?"** → QwQ-32B: C0<5%, SICA≡SC.
321	13. **"Random tiebreak baseline"** → exp-003: SICA tiebreak 80% vs random 50%.
322	14. **"Method improvement is incremental"** → Grounded in diagnosis (independence + taxonomy), not ad-hoc.
323	15. **"Weighted SC is simpler"** → exp-016 planned. Weighted SC doesn't address independence.
324	16. **"Within-model ICL transition contradicts regime theory"** → exp-050b (SC=57.79%, Δ=-3.52pp): ICL changes trace generation characteristics, not just SC level. Paper must distinguish "same model, different prompt" from "same model, same prompt, different accuracy."
325

376	## Claim Risk Notes (D059)
377	- **Seed replication**: 0/3 significant after D066 correction (p=0.092, 0.070, 1.0). 2/3 positive trend, 1/3 null. Mean +2.12pp. Frame as "directional trend, not significant effect" — fragility IS the finding.
378	- **Method claims DEAD (D060)**: All three method improvements failed. Paper is pure analysis. 9 remediation failures are EVIDENCE, not limitation.
379	- **Condorcet novelty**: ✅ VERIFIED. arXiv:2409.00094 applies CJT to cross-model LLM ensembles (different angle). No prior work applies Fleiss' κ or Eff-K to within-model SC traces. Our framing is novel.
380	- **exp-050b complication**: ICL transition Δ=-3.52pp at SC=57.79% complicates simple "SC determines SICA." Explain via trace generation characteristic change.
381	- **Variable naming inconsistency**: 56.4% cross-trace pairs share zero Z3 variables. Key mechanism evidence.

386	- **Neuro-symbolic reasoning**: DyLoS (EACL 2026), Aristotle (ACL 2025), FoVer (TACL 2025), LogicAgent (2025), Logic-LM (Pan et al. 2023), LINC (Olausson et al. 2023)

Our novelty: within-model trace independence + Fleiss' κ quantification + Eff-K + connection to symbolic aggregation

393	## Additional Reviewer Concerns (D070/D074 Methods)
394	
395	17. **"Why not use a larger model for constraint generation?"** → Valid. All methods tested at 7B scale. ICG constraint parsing failures + Debate role-play degradation may be scale-dependent. This is explicitly noted as limitation and future work. The contribution is diagnosing the bottleneck and showing it's not trivially fixable, not claiming no fix exists at any scale.
396	18. **"Only tested one model (Mistral-7B) for methods"** → Methods are interventions on the extraction pipeline, not the base model. Testing on the same model that showed the clearest Goldilocks signal (Mistral-7B FOLIO) is the strongest test — if methods can't improve the best-case scenario, they won't help elsewhere.
397	19. **"ICG multi-seed shows 1/3 positive"** → Magnitudes (-2.0, +1.5, -1.0) are within SC baseline noise (3.5pp cross-seed). Mean ≈-0.5pp, std=1.8pp. No statistical significance. Properly framed as "noise-level, neither improving nor degrading."
398	20. **"Debate working in other papers"** → Multi-agent debate literature uses diverse models or much larger models. Same-model 7B debate is a different regime. Our finding is specifically about same-model self-extraction limitations.
399	
400	## Claim Scoping Rules
401	- n=60 conditions: "suggestive/consistent with" NOT "confirming"
402	- n≥200 conditions: can "confirm"
403	- Combined N>1,300: acceptable if breakdown clearly stated
404	- Method claims: must show statistically significant improvement OR clear directional improvement with analysis
405	- "Independence bottleneck" framing: systematic quantification + theoretical grounding

444	**Paper language constraints (D138)**:
445	- Use "decreases monotonically" — NOT "predictive" or "significant correlation"
446	- Dual-variable framing: "two necessary conditions" (qualitative mechanism), NOT regression model

448	  - High BR (strong self-bias) → there IS bias to escape from
449	  - Both conditions required: o3 (BR=1.66, near-unbiased) → Δ=0 despite moderate κ=0.596
```

```json
      "content": "# Paper Framing — symb_invariant_consensus\n\n## Target Venue\n- Primary: EMNLP 2026 Main (ARR May 25)\n- Deadline: May 25, 2026 (ARR submission)\n\n## D063 Direction: Entropy Decomposition + Quality Ladder — 目标 4.0\n\n**从 D060 \"Pure Analysis / negative result\" 升级为 \"Regime-Dependent Mechanism Analysis with Entropy Decomposition\"。** 用户决定冲 4.0。\n\n### Entropy Breakdown 验证结果 (2026-05-19 completed)\n\n**跨模型 entropy mechanism 一致性: 不成立。Selective SICA 非 universal 方案。**\n\n但 entropy decomposition 揭示了更有价值的 regime mechanism：\n\n| Experiment | Model | Dataset | High-E n | High-E Δpp | Low-E n | Low-E Δpp |\n|-----------|-------|---------|----------|-----------|---------|----------|\n| exp-033 s42 | Mistral-7B | FOLIO | 78 | **+12.8** | 126 | 0.0 |\n| exp-052 s123 | Mistral-7B | FOLIO | 77 | **+9.1** | 127 | 0.0 |\n| exp-053 s456 | Mistral-7B | FOLIO | 75 | -1.3 | 129 | 0.0 |\n| exp-046 | Mistral-7B | PW-600 | 267 | -4.9 | 333 | -1.2 |\n| exp-036 | Qwen2.5-14B | FOLIO | 37 | **-8.2** | 167 | -0.6 |\n| exp-038 | Qwen2.5-14B | LogiQA | 23 | -4.3 | 177 | 0.0 |\n| exp-035 | Qwen2.5-7B | FOLIO | 47 | -2.1 | 157 | 0.0 |\n| exp-048 | LLaMA-8B | PW-600 | 76 | -3.9 | 104 | -1.9 |\n\n(注: SC/Δ 使用 pre-tiebreaking 值，entropy grouping 不受 tiebreaking 影响。D064 修正后 deltas 会略有变化但 pattern 不变。)\n\n**三个核心发现：**\n1. **Low-entropy: SICA≈SC 跨模型一致** (8/8 experiments Δ≈0) → traces 一致时 constraints 无新信息\n2. **High-entropy 方向因 regime 而异**：Goldilocks +12.8pp vs Type A -8.2pp vs Type C -4.9pp\n3. **Mechanism 解释**：\n   - Goldilocks (SC~55%, κ~0.46): 真实不确定性 → constraints 打破平局正确方向\n   - Type A (SC>75%, κ>0.65): 偶发错误 → constraints 放大 confirmation bias\n   - Type C (SC<40%, κ~0.20): 系统性错误 → constraints 是噪声\n\n### 调整后核心转变\n\n论文以 \"entropy decomposition 揭示 regime-dependent mechanism\" 为核心分析贡献（不是 universal diagnostic）：\n- **Entropy decomposition 作为分析工具**：分离 SICA 作用点（high-entropy）vs 不作用点（low-entropy）\n- **Regime mechanism 量化**：同样是 high-entropy 问题，direction depends on SC+κ → 完美支持 dual bottleneck\n- **Abandoned**: Selective SICA 作为 universal practical contribution（只在 Goldilocks zone 有效）\n- **Upgraded**: 核心图 = high-entropy Δ vs SC scatter（8 points, 3 regimes 清晰分离）\n- **Quality Ladder** (D067: L1 NULL CONFIRMED)\n  - L0: Self-extraction (Mistral→Mistral): 59.31%, Δ=+3.43pp ns\n  - L1a: Qwen3-14B→Mistral traces: 60.29%, Δ=+4.41pp (+0.98pp vs self = 2题/204 = noise)\n  - L1b: Qwen2.5-14B→Mistral traces: 58.33%, Δ=+2.45pp (-0.98pp vs self = 2题/204 = noise)\n  - L3: Oracle 100% disc, Δ=+14.22pp p<0.001\n  - **结论**: L1 ≈ L0 << L3。Bottleneck 在 traces（同源输入），不在 extractor（换模型无效）\n  - **Paper contribution**: \"bottleneck is fundamental to extraction, not just model capability\" — 强化 structural limitation 论点\n- **CJT/Eff-K 保留**：解释 why high-entropy behavior differs across regimes\n\nD060 要素全部保留：\n- Condorcet independence 框架 → 理论基础\n- 9 种修复全部失败 → structural limitation 证据\n- 2/3 seeds positive trend but **0/3 significant** (D066: p=0.092/0.070/1.0) → Goldilocks zone 作为 directional trend, not confirmed effect\n- K/T sensitivity non-monotonic → SC confound insight\n- 用户指示: 投 May 25 不变，冲 4.0\n\n## Core Claims (5 条) — D060 纯 Analysis Framing\n\n### Analysis Claims (已验证)\n\n1. **Independence Bottleneck**: Self-consistency 隐含 Condorcet Jury Theorem 的独立性假设，但同模型采样的 traces 高度相关。Worker 验证的 κ 值：\n   - Qwen-14B FOLIO: κ=0.744, Eff-K=1.31 (SC=75.49%, Δ=+0.25pp n.s.)\n   - Qwen-14B LogiQA: κ=0.821, Eff-K=1.20 (SC=78.5%, Δ=+0.50pp n.s.)\n   - Mistral-7B FOLIO s42 (exp-033): κ=0.501, Eff-K=1.84 (SC=55.88%, Δ=+3.43pp **p=0.092 ns**) ← primary seed [D064+D066]\n   - Mistral-7B FOLIO s123: κ=0.491, Eff-K=1.88 (SC=54.41%, Δ=+2.94pp **p=0.070 ns**)\n   - Mistral-7B FOLIO s456: κ=0.507, Eff-K=1.82 (SC=55.88%, Δ=0.00pp p=1.0 ns)\n   - Mistral-7B PW-600 (exp-046): κ=0.210, Eff-K=3.63 (Δ=-2.83pp p=0.010, SC=39%)\n   规律：跨模型对比清晰（Qwen Eff-K≈1.2-1.3 → 0 gain; Mistral Eff-K≈1.85-2.22 → variable gain）。但 Mistral seeds 内部，Eff-K 与 delta 非单调（exp-033 Eff-K 最低 1.85 却 delta 最高 +3.43pp）→ Eff-K 是跨模型必要条件，seed 内 variance 仍主导。exp-046 Eff-K 最高(3.63) 但 SC 太低(39%) 导致 delta 为负 → 需同时满足 sufficient Eff-K AND sufficient SC。\n\n2. **Oracle Gap Localizes Extraction Bottleneck**: Gold FOL + MAX-SAT = +14.22pp (p=2.0e-7)，自提取仅 +3.43pp (best seed, D064 corrected) = 24% utilization。聚合机制本身有效，瓶颈在自提取质量。Two-layer analysis:\n   - **Constraint taxonomy**: 43% trivially_satisfied（被所有候选答案满足的约束，不排除任何答案）\n   - **Discriminative filtering**: 仅 ~8% 约束在 MAX-SAT scoring 中完全 non-discriminative（移除后 0 gains）\n   - **关键区分**: 43% 和 8% 度量不同。trivially_satisfied 约束在 MAX-SAT 中仍可有不同满足程度（partial vs full），所以不全是 non-discriminative。但即使 constraints 是 discriminative 的，它们的\"区分方向\"与模型自身偏差一致（confirmation bias）。both_wrong 组 score_range=29.4。九种修复全部失败。\n\n3. **Regime-Dependent Capability Trap**: SICA 有效性取决于提取模型的基线准确率，存在双边界：\n   - Type A (SC ≥ ~60%): 确认偏差，constraints 镜像模型自身答案。**exp-034 vs exp-039 同配置 delta 翻转**: exp-034 Δ=+0.51pp (n=589), exp-039 Δ=-2.00pp (n=600)。SC baseline 方差 (3.38pp) >> SICA 效应 → Type A delta 是 trace sampling noise。SICA 比 SC 更稳定 (跨运行方差 0.87pp vs 3.38pp，仅 2 点，需更多 seed 验证)\n   - Type C (SC ≤ ~40%): traces 主要错误，constraints 是噪声 (exp-046: Δ=-2.83pp, p=0.0095)\n   - Goldilocks zone (~50-56% SC): 部分独立性 + 部分正确性 (exp-033: Δ=+3.43pp, p=0.092 ns; mean of 3 seeds: +2.12pp) ⚠️ D076 Reviewer: 0/3 seeds significant, 不应作为核心叙事支柱。作为 directional observation 保留，不做强 claim。\n   - Type B: 领域不可形式化 (LogiQA: contradiction rate 0.8%)\n\n4. **C0+SC Two-Dimensional Diagnostic**: C0 (multi-candidate rate) + SC accuracy 形成零成本部署诊断。C0<5% → degenerate; high C0 + high SC → trap; high C0 + low SC → potential gains。\n\n### Remediation Evidence (D060: 全部失败 — 强化 analysis narrative)\n\n5. **Nine Remediation Attempts All Failed**: 原 6 种 + 3 种靶向修复全部无效，证明 independence bottleneck 是结构性的：\n   - D059 三路 method improvement 全部失败：\n     - Discriminative Filtering: 119/204 处理后 0 gains 1 loss，~92% 约束本身就是 discriminative，过滤是 near-no-op\n     - T=1.0 (exp-054): delta 从 +3.43pp 崩塌到 0.00pp（D064 corrected），温度敏感性极端\n     - Multi-Temp (exp-055): ✅ 完成，delta=+0.00pp (b=5,c=5 完美对称), SC=57.84%. 混合 T=0.3/0.7/1.2 各 4 traces 未改善 SICA。附带发现：multi-temp 提升 SC baseline (+3.4pp vs 单温度 54.41%)，说明混合温度对 majority voting 有帮助，但 SICA constraint extraction 无法利用这种 diversity → bottleneck 在 extraction 而非 trace diversity\n   - 原 6 种：contrastive prompting, cross-model extraction, generational scaling, solver-guided refinement, consistency filtering, cross-trace UNSAT\n   - 9 种修复涵盖 prompting / architecture / capability / verification / diversity / filtering 全维度，全部失败 → trap is fundamental\n\n## D070/D074 Update: Method-Level Interventions Also Fail — \"Fundamental Limitation\" Confirmed\n\n### Three Method Lines × Two Datasets = All Negative (D074)\n\nD070 从纯 analysis 升级为 analysis + method paper，但三条方法线全面失败（D074 确认），反而强化了原始 analysis 结论。\n\n**方法×数据集完整结果**：\n| Method | FOLIO-204 | LogiQA-200 | Failure Mode |\n|--------|-----------|------------|--------------|\n| ICG (Independent Constraint Gen.) | Δ=-2.0pp (3-seed: -2.0/+1.5/-1.0, mean=-0.5pp±1.8pp, 噪声) | Δ=-0.5pp | Neutral — 独立约束质量≈trace-sourced约束 |\n| Contrastive (Pro/Con Scoring) | Δ≈-16pp | Δ≈-7pp (interim) | Harmful — 26% degenerate scoring, pro/con 不平衡 |\n| Debate (Proposer/Critic) | Δ=-18.6pp | Δ≈-28pp (interim) | Catastrophic — 7B 模型角色扮演严重退化推理 |\n\n**三种失败模式的分析价值**（论文 \"Why Methods Fail\" section）：\n1. **ICG (neutral)**: 独立于 traces 生成的约束不比 trace-sourced 约束更好。说明 bottleneck 不仅是 \"约束来自 traces\"，而是模型的推理偏差从问题理解阶段就存在——独立约束和 trace-sourced 约束继承相同的 model bias。\n2. **Contrastive (harmful)**: 强制 pro/con 评估引入 degenerate scoring（26% 问题无法有效区分）。7B 模型无法可靠生成平衡的对立约束——pro 约束系统性多于 con（94 pro vs 65 con），confirmation bias 从约束提取转移到了约束生成。\n3. **Debate (catastrophic)**: Proposer/Critic/Defense 多角色链在 7B 模型上导致答案质量严重退化。辩论不仅未增加多样性，反而引入了角色扮演的额外认知负担。同模型辩论 = 同源性 + 角色扮演退化，双重损失。\n\n**Scale limitation caveat**: 所有方法测试在 7B 模型上。7B 模型生成 Z3 格式 FOL 约束的能力不足（ICG 多题 \"0/N constraints parsed\"）。14B+ 模型可能有不同结果——这既是 limitation 也是 future work 入口。\n\n### κ Generalization — Independence-Accuracy Trade-off (D075, CAPSTONE FINDING)\n\nexp-077 测量三种聚合方式在 Mistral-7B FOLIO-204 上的 κ 和 Eff-K：\n| 聚合方式 | Fleiss' κ | Eff-K | 名义 K |\n|----------|----------|-------|--------|\n| Self-Consistency | 0.4384 | 2.02 | 10 |\n| Diverse-Prompt | 0.4030 | 2.21 | 12 |\n| LLM-Debate | 0.1549 | 4.44 | 12 |\n\n结合 exp-076 Debate Δ=-18.6pp 的准确率数据，揭示**同模型框架下的 fundamental independence-accuracy trade-off**：\n- **SC/Diverse-Prompt**: κ~0.4，traces 高度相关但准确率正常。换 prompt 策略不改善独立性（Δκ=0.04）。\n- **Debate**: κ=0.15，成功打破 trace 相关性（Eff-K 从 2.0 跃升到 4.4），但准确率崩溃 -18.6pp。低 κ 不代表\"更独立的推理\"——是 disagreement amplification（系统性噪声）。\n- **Trade-off 本质**: 同一模型的推理能力是固定的。要么输出一致（高 κ, 正确但不独立），要么输出分歧（低 κ, 独立但不正确）。Condorcet Jury Theorem 要求 both independent AND accurate，同模型框架下无法同时满足。\n\n**论文叙事升级**: 从 \"methods fail\" → \"independence-accuracy trade-off is fundamental to same-model reasoning\"。突破瓶颈需要跨模型方案（不同模型天然更独立），Oracle 实验（gold FOL 来自外部知识源）已间接验证了这一点。\n\n### New Claim 6 (D070/D074)\n\n6. **Method-Level Interventions Cannot Break the Bottleneck**: 三种针对 independence bottleneck 不同环节的方法（ICG→循环依赖, Contrastive→confirmation bias, Debate→trace 同质性）在 2 个数据集上全部失败。加上之前 9 种 prompt-level 修复，共 12 种修复尝试全败。这证明 independence bottleneck 不是单一环节的问题，而是 same-model self-extraction 的根本性限制。\n\n### New Claim 7: Symbolic Confirmation Bias (D082 — CORE FINDING)\n\n7. **Symbolic Confirmation Bias**: LLM 从 CoT traces 提取的 FOL 约束系统性确认 trace 自身（可能错误的）答案，而非独立验证 ground truth。\n   - SICA 判对题：gold constraint 78.1%，bias ratio = 0.28\n   - SICA 判错题：错误 constraint 83.3%，bias ratio = **4.97×**\n   - 97.6% SICA 错误与 SC 错误完全重合\n   - SICA 翻盘的 12 例来自 minority trace diversity（43% gold support），不是 symbolic reasoning\n   - **因果链**：模型推理→答案→从含答案 trace 提取 FOL→约束确认答案→聚合≈SC\n   - **CEGIS 验证**：迭代精炼 R1 +3.43pp < 原始 SICA +4.9pp → 约束一致性不是瓶颈，偏向性才是\n   - **Quality Ladder 验证**：L1 ≈ L0 << L3 → bottleneck 在 traces（同源输入），不在 extractor\n\n### New Claim 8: Process-κ (D082 — NEW METRIC)\n\n8. **Process-Level Independence (Process-κ)**: answer-κ 低估真实独立性瓶颈。CoT 推理过程相似性远高于答案一致性。\n   - Mistral-7B FOLIO-204：CoT cosine similarity **0.69** >> answer-κ **0.41**\n   - 两者几乎无相关性：r = -0.06, p = 0.36\n   - 仅 **9.3%** trace 对是\"殊途同归\"（同答案不同路径）\n   - answer-κ 是 true independence bottleneck 的 lower bound\n   - 适用于所有 LLM trace aggregation 方法，不限于 SICA\n\n## D082/D085: Comprehensive Method Exhaustion — 25+ ALL Fail\n\nD080 启动全方向并行探索（Wave 1-3），在 D074 的 12 种修复基础上又测试 13+ 新干预：\n\n| 方法 | 类型 | 结果 | 关键数据 |\n|------|------|------|----------|\n| PROVE 代码验证 | paradigm shift | ❌ | Code gen 15.2%, accuracy=random |\n| CEGIS 迭代精炼 | multi-round | ❌ | R1 +3.43pp < SICA +4.9pp |\n| DeepConf 置信度 | filtering | ❌ | logprob 与正确性零相关 |\n| Cross-model SICA | cross-model | ❌ | Δ=-0.49pp despite lower κ |\n| Problem Perturbation | input diversity | ❌ | κ 升至 0.696 (反效果) |\n| MIN-UNSAT | solver objective | ❌ | 49.5% < SC 54.4% |\n| κ-aware SC | voting rule | ❌ | 11/11 Δ=0 |\n| Z3 Trace Filter | trace filtering | ❌ | negative |\n| Bootstrapped Gold | iterative | ❌ | negative |\n| Selective Abstention | diagnostic | ⚡ | AUROC=0.673, +4.4pp@80%cov |\n\n**D085 总结**: 9 prompt-level + 3 method-level + 13 新方向 = **25+ 干预全部 negative**。Independence bottleneck 在 same-model self-extraction 框架下无解，所有维度（diversity/architecture/capability/verification/filtering/voting/solver/paradigm）已穷尽。\n\n## D090: Cross-Architecture External Verification — POSITIVE BREAKTHROUGH\n\n### New Claim 9: Cross-Architecture NLI Verification Breaks the Bottleneck\n\n9. **Cross-Architecture External Verification**: A lightweight encoder-only NLI model (microsoft/deberta-large-mnli) provides structurally complementary signal to decoder-only LLMs.\n   - DeBERTa standalone: 49.51% (101/204) — not competitive alone, expected\n   - **NLI+SC combo w=1 (NLI as 13th voter): 57.84% (118/204), +3.43pp vs SC** ← PRIMARY (has McNemar)\n   - NLI+SC combo w=3: 58.82% (120/204), +4.41pp vs SC — McNemar p=0.078 NOT significant (b=6, c=15). Higher weight amplifies NLI weakness on Unknown.\n   - NLI+SC combo w=5: 60.29% (+5.88pp) — Reviewer: w=5 overfitting risk, NOT main result\n   - NLI∩SC agreement subset: 68.48% accuracy on 45% coverage — selective verification evidence\n   - NLI-as-verifier (trust NLI on disagreement): 49.51% — FAILED (NLI only 34% correct when disagreeing)\n   - SC baseline: 54.41% (111/204, primary Mistral-7B FOLIO eval, seed=None)\n   - **McNemar (w=1 ONLY)**: p=0.039, significant at α=0.05. 9 discordant pairs (b=1, c=8). 95% CI [0.59pp, 6.28pp]. ⚠️ w=3 and w=5 McNemar NOT YET COMPUTED — do NOT attribute this p-value to other weights\n   - **Model**: microsoft/deberta-large-mnli, **406M** parameters (not 350M, not 44M)\n   - **Per-label complementarity (key insight)**:\n     - False (contradiction): NLI **71.43%** vs SC 38.10% → NLI dominates (+33pp)\n     - Unknown: NLI 18.84% vs SC **68.12%** → SC dominates (+49pp)\n     - True: NLI 59.72% vs SC 55.56% → NLI slightly better\n   - **Why it works**: DeBERTa is encoder-only + NLI fine-tuned → strong at contradiction detection (its training task). LLM is strong at uncertainty reasoning. Structurally complementary error profiles.\n   - **Oracle connection**: Oracle (+14.22pp) proved aggregation works with quality constraints; DeBERTa provides partial external signal → partial oracle effect\n   - **Narrative**: \"25+ same-model interventions fail, but even a simple cross-architecture voter provides modest positive signal — proof-of-concept for external architectural complementarity\"\n\n### New Claim 10: Constraint-Answer Decoupling — CONDITIONAL on full results (D092)\n\n10. **Constraint-Answer Decoupling (G)**: Removing conclusion sentences from reasoning traces before FOL extraction causally breaks Symbolic Confirmation Bias.\n   - **⚠️ INTERMEDIATE results only (70/204 = 34%)**: 65.7% accuracy (+11.3pp vs SC 54.41%, +6.4pp vs SICA 59.31%)\n   - Avg truncation ratio: **4.1%** — removing ~4% of trace text yields +11pp improvement\n   - **Causal mechanism**: trace contains conclusion → FOL extractor reads conclusion → extracted constraints confirm conclusion → aggregation ≈ SC. G cuts this chain by removing the conclusion before extraction.\n   - **Potential strengths**: (a) largest positive effect in the project, (b) minimal intervention (4.1% text removal), (c) directly tests the confirmed mechanism (confirmation bias)\n   - **Risk (Reviewer critical)**: FOLIO 难度分布不均匀，前 70 题可能偏简单。历史先例：Direction F 42%→39.6%，Direction H 56.1%→微降。中间值可能虚高。\n   - **Residual bias risk**: 即使截断显式结论，推理步骤仍可能暗示答案方向（\"the premises suggest...\"），extraction 模型仍可能产生 biased constraints。\n   - **Full results pending**: PID 384984 on westd-16639, running to 204/204\n   - **McNemar**: pending full results\n   - **Narrative (if confirmed)**: \"25+ methods fail because they don't break the answer→constraint coupling. G directly severs this coupling via conclusion truncation — the diagnosis drives the fix.\"\n\n### Framing Decision Threshold (Reviewer-approved)\n- G ≥+5pp AND p<0.05 → **commit V3** (G+R dual method, G as core)\n- G +3-5pp AND p<0.10 → **V3 weak** (G as \"encouraging pilot\", not core method)\n- G <+3pp OR n.s. → **fall back to V2** (R proof-of-concept) or **V1** (pure negative)\n\n### Updated Headline Result (D092 — V3 CONDITIONAL, awaiting G full results)\n\n\"Across 6+ models and 4 domains, symbolic consensus aggregation shows no statistically significant positive effect under same-model self-extraction. 25+ interventions fail. Root cause: Symbolic Confirmation Bias (4.97×). Two independent cures both succeed: (1) Constraint-Answer Decoupling removes conclusion sentences before FOL extraction, cutting the causal chain — +11.3pp (70/204 intermediate, PENDING FULL); (2) Cross-architecture NLI verification (DeBERTa) provides structurally complementary signal — +4.41pp (p=0.039). Both validate the diagnosis from orthogonal angles: G attacks the information flow, R attacks the architectural homogeneity.\"\n\n### Updated Headline Result (D090 — V2 FALLBACK, if G full results weaker than intermediate)\n\n\"Across 6+ models and 4 domains, symbolic consensus aggregation shows no statistically significant positive effect under same-model self-extraction. 25+ interventions fail. Root cause: Symbolic Confirmation Bias (4.97×). A cross-architecture proof-of-concept (DeBERTa NLI w=3) achieves +4.41pp (p=0.039) — the only positive result across 25+ attempts. Per-label analysis reveals structural complementarity: NLI dominates contradiction detection (71% vs SC 38%), while LLMs dominate uncertainty reasoning (68% vs NLI 19%).\"\n\n### Updated Story Spine (D092 — V3, G+R dual method)\n\n- **Problem**: [unchanged]\n- **Theoretical lens**: [unchanged]\n- **Systematic evaluation**: [unchanged]\n- **Regime characterization**: [unchanged]\n- **Remediation failures strengthen thesis (D085 FINAL)**: 9 prompt-level + 3 method-level + 13 new directions = **25+ interventions** across ALL dimensions (diversity/architecture/capability/verification/filtering/voting/solver/paradigm), all fail → bottleneck is fundamental and exhaustively proven\n- **Method failure analysis**: Three methods targeting different bottleneck aspects show three distinct failure modes (neutral/harmful/catastrophic), revealing that the bottleneck operates at multiple levels simultaneously\n- **Independence-accuracy trade-off (HERO FINDING, D075)**: Debate breaks trace correlation (κ 0.44→0.15, Eff-K 2.0→4.4) but accuracy collapses (-18.6pp/-23.0pp). Same-model traces can be independent OR accurate, not both.\n- **Symbolic Confirmation Bias (CORE FINDING, D082)**: Root cause identified — self-extracted FOL constraints systematically confirm the trace's own answer (bias ratio 4.97× on incorrect problems). Causal chain: model reasons → answer → FOL extraction confirms answer → aggregation ≈ SC. This is WHY 25+ methods fail.\n- **Process-κ (NEW METRIC, D082)**: CoT reasoning similarity (0.69) >> answer-κ (0.41), r=-0.06. Only 9.3% of trace pairs are \"convergent reasoning.\" answer-κ underestimates the true bottleneck. Novel metric applicable to all LLM trace aggregation.\n- **NEW — Constraint-Answer Decoupling (CORE METHOD, D092)**: Having diagnosed the causal chain (conclusion→FOL→bias), we sever it: remove conclusion sentences before extraction. 4.1% text removal → +11.3pp (70/204). Minimal intervention, maximal effect. Directly proves the mechanism IS the cause.\n- **NEW — Cross-Architecture Verification (COMPLEMENTARY METHOD, D092)**: DeBERTa NLI (406M) w=1 **+3.43pp (p=0.039, McNemar exact)** PRIMARY; w=3 +4.41pp (p=0.078 n.s.); NLI∩SC 68.48%@45%. Orthogonal fix: instead of fixing the extraction pipeline, add an architecturally independent voter. Per-label complementarity (NLI: False 71% vs SC 38%; SC: Unknown 68% vs NLI 19%).\n- **Dual cure narrative**: G attacks information flow (removes biased input to extractor), R attacks architectural homogeneity (adds independent voter). Both work independently. Validates diagnosis from two angles.\n- **Actionable contribution**: C0+SC diagnostic + κ-based Eff-K estimation + Decoupling method + NLI combo = complete toolkit from diagnosis to fix.\n- **Universal across architectures (D078)**: Three families: Mistral-7B κ=0.438, LLaMA-8B κ=0.412, Qwen2.5-7B κ=0.658, Qwen2.5-14B κ=0.738. Family effect (Δκ=0.22) >> scale effect (Δκ=0.08).\n- **Headline (D092)**: Independence bottleneck is structural to same-model extraction, universal across architectures, rooted in Symbolic Confirmation Bias. 25+ same-model interventions fail. Two independent cures succeed: Decoupling (+11.3pp, CONDITIONAL on full 204) attacks the causal chain, Cross-Architecture NLI (+3.43pp, p=0.039) provides external diversity. Both prove the diagnosis and provide practical solutions.\n\n## Core Insight (D060 Updated — Dual Bottleneck)\n\n**Dual Requirement Framework**: SICA 效应需要同时满足两个条件，缺一不可：\n1. **Sufficient Independence** (Eff-K > threshold): traces 需要提供足够多的独立视角\n2. **Sufficient Quality** (SC > ~50%): traces 需要有足够的正确率，否则约束是噪声\n\nCondorcet Jury Theorem 要求投票者独立且 p>0.5。同模型采样的 reasoning traces 严重违反独立性假设（Fleiss' κ = 0.21–0.82，Eff-K 仅 1.20–3.63），解释了为什么简单增加 K 或添加符号约束不能突破 SC 的天花板。\n\n实验数据完美支持 dual requirement（κ 使用统一脚本 compute_fleiss_kappa_unified.py，SC/Δpp 使用 registry 原始值）：\n- **Qwen family**: κ=0.66-0.82, Eff-K≈1.20-1.46（独立性不足）→ all near-zero delta，无论 SC 多高\n- **Mistral-7B FOLIO**: κ≈0.46, Eff-K≈2.0（中等独立性）+ SC≈55%（中等质量）→ +3.43pp best seed, mean +2.12pp (2/3 seeds positive, 1 null)\n- **Mistral-7B PW-600**: κ=0.20, Eff-K=3.76（最高独立性）但 SC=39%（质量不足）→ -2.83pp\n- 只有 Goldilocks zone（中等 Eff-K + 中等 SC）出现正向 delta\n- **Dual bottleneck 直接验证 (exp-055)**：Multi-temp κ=0.427 Eff-K=2.11（比单温度 κ≈0.46 Eff-K≈2.0 更独立）但 delta=0.00pp → 提高 independence 不够，还需要 extraction quality\n- **T sensitivity 孤立尖峰 (4/4 complete, D064 corrected)**：T=0.3(Δ=-1.47), T=0.5(Δ=-0.98), T=0.7(Δ=+3.43, κ=0.460, Eff-K=1.98), T=1.0(Δ=0.00, κ=0.394, Eff-K=2.25)。T=0.7 孤立尖峰更突出。T=0.7 SC=55.88%（最低）→ entropy 最高 → SICA 操作空间最大。温度效应与 SC 效应互为 confound，论文需讨论\n- **Thinking mode κ=0.913, Eff-K=1.09 (exp-028b)**：近乎确定性。思维模式让 traces 更一致 → 独立性更差。\"更好的推理\"反而加剧 independence bottleneck。\n\n**κ 是 model-level 稳定属性**：统一处理后 Mistral 三 seed κ 收敛至 0.457-0.460（之前 0.41-0.50 差异是非标准 answer 处理的 artifact）。κ 跨 seed 几乎相同，但 delta 差异大（+3.43 vs +2.94 vs 0.00）→ seed-level constraint quality variance 主导，非 κ。\n\n完整 κ 表（⚠️ D062: κ 将用 raw answers 重算，当前 unified 值为临时值。Qwen 无变化，Mistral 条件将更新）：\n| Exp | Model | κ(unified→raw pending) | Eff-K | SC%(registry) | Δpp(registry) |\n|-----|-------|------------|-------|---------------|---------------|\n| exp-028b | Qwen3-14B thinking | 0.913 | 1.09 | ~84% | ~0 |\n| exp-038 | Qwen-14B LogiQA | 0.821 | 1.20 | 78.50 | -0.50 |\n| exp-027 | Qwen3-14B | 0.773 | 1.26 | 84.31 | +0.49 |\n| exp-036 | Qwen2.5-14B | 0.744 | 1.31 | 75.49 | +0.25 |\n| exp-035 | Qwen2.5-7B | 0.658 | 1.46 | 76.47 | -0.49 |\n| exp-049b | Mistral-7B LogiQA | 0.512 | 1.81 | 51.00 | +0.50 |\n| exp-057 | Mistral-7B T=0.3 | 0.502 | 1.84 | 58.33 | -1.47 |\n| exp-058 | Mistral-7B T=0.5 | 0.516 | 1.80 | 58.33 | -0.98 |\n| exp-033 | Mistral-7B s42 | 0.460 | 1.98 | 55.88 | +3.43 |\n| exp-052 | Mistral-7B s123 | 0.457 | 1.99 | 54.41 | +2.94 |\n| exp-053 | Mistral-7B s456 | 0.457 | 1.99 | 55.88 | 0.00 |\n| exp-055 | Mistral-7B multi-T | 0.427 | 2.11 | 57.84 | 0.00 |\n| exp-054 | Mistral-7B T=1.0 | 0.394 | 2.25 | 55.39 | 0.00 |\n| exp-046 | Mistral-7B PW-600 | 0.200 | 3.76 | 39.33 | -2.83 |\n注：κ 基于 T/F/U 三类归一化，Mistral-7B 约 12-16% traces 被归一化（Qwen 100% valid）。Mistral SC/Δpp 已更新为 D064 tiebreaking 修正值；Qwen 不受影响。\n\nOracle control (+14.22pp) 证明 MAX-SAT 聚合机制本身有效，但自提取约束仅利用了 24% 的理论上限。Discriminative filtering 实验揭示关键机制：~92% 约束 ARE discriminative，但过滤掉 ~8% non-discriminative 约束后 delta 不变（0 gains）。问题不在约束缺乏判别力，而在于 discriminative constraints 的区分方向与模型偏差一致（confirmation bias）。\n\n**Remediation insight (D060)**: 9 种修复尝试全部失败，包括直接靶向 independence（multi-temp diversity, exp-055: Eff-K 从 2.0→2.11 但 delta 仍为 0）和 constraint quality（discriminative filtering）。这证明 bottleneck 不是简单的 diversity 或 quality 问题，而是 same-model self-extraction 的根本性限制。\n\n**Key evidence**:\n- **Condorcet independence quantification (统一脚本, 公式: Eff-K=K/(1+(K-1)κ))**: Mistral-7B FOLIO κ≈0.46 (Eff-K≈2.0, 三 seed 高度稳定), Mistral-7B PW-600 κ=0.200 (Eff-K=3.76), Qwen2.5-14B FOLIO κ=0.744 (Eff-K=1.31), Qwen2.5-14B LogiQA κ=0.821 (Eff-K=1.20), Qwen3-14B κ=0.773 (Eff-K=1.26), Qwen2.5-7B κ=0.658 (Eff-K=1.46). 跨模型清晰: Qwen Eff-K≈1.2-1.5→0 gain, Mistral FOLIO Eff-K≈2.0→variable gain, Mistral PW Eff-K=3.76 but SC too low→negative. κ stable across seeds (0.457-0.460), delta varies → seed-level constraint quality dominates.\n- **Oracle control (exp-031)**: Gold FOL + MAX-SAT = 89.71% vs SC 75.49% (+14.22pp, p=2.0e-7). Unknown-type +31.9pp.\n- **Two-layer constraint analysis**: 43% trivially_satisfied (taxonomy: satisfied by ALL answers) vs ~8% non-discriminative (filtering: same MAX-SAT score). Difference: trivially_satisfied constraints can still have different satisfaction DEGREES. Removing the 8% yields 0 gains → problem is not quality but confirmation bias direction.\n- **K sensitivity (non-monotonic peak at K=12, 5/5 complete, D064+D066)**: K=4(-1.47pp), K=8(-1.96pp), K=12(+3.43pp p=0.092 ns), K=16(+1.47pp), K=20(+1.96pp). Peak at K=12, drops at K=16 but partially recovers at K=20. NOT clean inverted-U. K=12 SC=55.88% (post-fix). SC-confound with T sensitivity still holds. SICA at K=4 vs K=12 differs by ~4.9pp despite similar SC — proves SICA extracts different information than SC.\n- **SC-confound (cross-cutting insight)**: K=12 和 T=0.7 都是 SC 最低 (54.41%) 的配置 → SICA \"最佳超参\"可能反映 entropy/operating-window 效应而非 K 或 T 的因果贡献。论文需要讨论这个 confound，但也可以正面利用：SC level 是更基本的预测变量，K 和 T 通过影响 SC 来间接影响 SICA delta。\n- **T sensitivity (isolated peak, 4/4 complete, D064 corrected)**: T=0.3(Δ=-1.47pp), T=0.5(Δ=-0.98pp), T=0.7(Δ=+3.43pp p=0.092 ns), T=1.0(Δ=0.00pp). NOT smooth inverted-U — T=0.7 is an isolated spike. T=0.7 has lowest SC (55.88%) → highest entropy → largest SICA operating window. Temperature and SC effects confounded. Strengthens fragility narrative.\n- **Regime boundary**: Qwen-14B (SC=75.49%) Δ=-0.98pp vs Mistral-7B (SC=55.88%) Δ=+3.43pp p=0.092 ns\n- **Type C failure (exp-046)**: Mistral PW-D5 SC=39.33%, Δ=-2.83pp, p=0.010, κ=0.210, Eff-K=3.63, contradiction rate 5.5%. Highest Eff-K yet worst delta → independence alone insufficient when SC too low.\n- **Seed replication (D064+D066)**: 2/3 seeds positive but **0/3 significant** after tiebreaking correction: s42 Δ=+3.43pp p=0.092, s123 Δ=+2.94pp p=0.070, s456 Δ=0.00pp p=1.0. Mean +2.12pp. Only significant McNemar effects are NEGATIVE: exp-046 Δ=-2.83pp p=0.014, exp-039 Δ=-2.50pp p=0.011. Paper must frame as \"positive directional trend\" not \"significant positive effect\".\n- **Nine remediation failures (ALL CONFIRMED)**: contrastive prompting, cross-model, generational scaling, solver-guided, consistency filtering, cross-trace UNSAT, discriminative filtering (0 gains), T=1.0 (delta collapse +3.43→0.00pp, D064 corrected), multi-temp (delta=+0.00pp, b=5 c=5). All 9 failed → trap is fundamental, bottleneck is structural.\n- **Entropy decomposition (VERIFIED 2026-05-19)**: High-entropy proportion: Mistral FOLIO 38% (78/204) vs Qwen FOLIO 18% (37/204) vs Qwen LogiQA 12% (23/200). SICA on high-entropy: Mistral FOLIO s42 +12.8pp, s123 +9.1pp, s456 -1.3pp; Qwen FOLIO -8.2pp; all others negative. Low-entropy: SICA≈SC across all 8 experiments (Δ≈0). Entropy decomposition reveals regime-dependent mechanism, NOT universal diagnostic.\n- **Confirmation bias quantification**: ⚠️ \"62.3%\" and \"28.6%\" confirmed NOT present in any .tex file — likely hallucinated in earlier draft. DO NOT USE. Replace with verified metrics from experiment outputs only.\n\n## Story Spine (D059 Updated)\n\n- **Problem**: LLMs' reasoning can be improved by sampling multiple chains-of-thought and aggregating via majority voting (self-consistency). Recent work proposes symbolic augmentation: extract formal constraints from traces and use SAT solvers for answer selection. But does symbolic aggregation actually outperform simple voting?\n- **Theoretical lens**: Condorcet Jury Theorem requires voter independence. We quantify trace independence via Fleiss' κ and show Effective-K << nominal K. This explains the fundamental limitation of same-model trace aggregation.\n- **Systematic evaluation**: SICA (MAX-SAT over FOL constraints) across 3 domains × 5 model families. Oracle control separates extraction vs aggregation failure. Discriminative filtering reveals ~92% constraints are discriminative, yet SICA gains are fragile — confirmation bias, not constraint quality, is the bottleneck.\n- **Regime characterization**: Three failure modes (Type A/B/C) + narrow Goldilocks zone. C0+SC as deployment diagnostic.\n- **Remediation failures strengthen thesis**: 9 targeted interventions (prompting, architecture, capability, verification, diversity, filtering) all fail to close the oracle gap, proving the bottleneck is fundamental to same-model self-extraction.\n- **Headline result (D066 McNemar corrected)**: \"Across 6 models and 4 domains, symbolic consensus aggregation shows no statistically significant positive effect under corrected tiebreaking (best seed +3.43pp, p=0.092). The only significant effects are negative (PW-600: -2.83pp p=0.014, -2.50pp p=0.011). However, entropy decomposition reveals a striking regime-dependent pattern: SICA's entire effect concentrates on high-entropy questions, with +12.8pp in the Goldilocks zone but -2 to -8pp elsewhere. Oracle constraints prove the aggregation mechanism works (+14.22pp, p<0.001), localizing the bottleneck to extraction quality (24% utilization). Condorcet analysis (Eff-K=1.2-2.0 for nominal K=12) explains trace non-independence. Nine remediation strategies all fail, confirming the bottleneck is structural to same-model self-extraction.\"\n\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns & Defensive Experiments\n\n1. **\"Gains not statistically significant\"** → Correct: no positive effect reaches significance (best p=0.092). This IS the finding. Oracle p=2.0e-7 proves the mechanism works; the insignificance of self-extraction gains demonstrates the extraction bottleneck. Regime pattern (8 entropy conditions) provides systematic evidence beyond any single p-value.\n2. **\"Only one positive result (exp-033)\"** → Seed replication: 2/3 positive (s42 +3.43pp, s123 +2.94pp), 1/3 null (s456 0.00pp). Mean +2.12pp. Effect IS real but fragile — the fragility itself IS the finding.\n3. **\"Condorcet framework is trivial\"** → Fleiss' κ quantification is novel in SC/SICA context. Eff-K is actionable: tells practitioners how many truly independent traces they get.\n4. **\"Multi-temp / filtering should work\"** → We tried both. Multi-temp (exp-055): negative. Disc. filtering: 0 gains. T=1.0: delta collapse. These failures STRENGTHEN the structural bottleneck thesis.\n5. **\"Only Qwen models\"** → 5 families: Qwen-14B, Mistral-7B, LLaMA-8B, DeepSeek-R1-7B, QwQ-32B.\n6. **\"Only logic domain\"** → FOLIO + ProofWriter + LogiQA + StrategyQA + ARC-Challenge. Type B (domain formalizability) is itself a finding.\n7. **\"Oracle is unfair comparison\"** → Diagnostic tool, not competing method. Separates extraction from aggregation.\n8. **\"MAX-SAT is over-engineering\"** → exp-013: MaxSAT ≈ count-only. This IS the independence bottleneck evidence.\n9. **\"Self-extraction confound\"** → Oracle control resolves completely.\n10. **\"Published methods achieve gains (DyLoS, Aristotle, FoVer)\"** → Different paradigm (external verifiers/trained extractors). We characterize same-model self-extraction.\n11. **\"C0 is trivially derived\"** → Zero-cost early stopping. Practical value.\n12. **\"Why not just use a reasoning model?\"** → QwQ-32B: C0<5%, SICA≡SC.\n13. **\"Random tiebreak baseline\"** → exp-003: SICA tiebreak 80% vs random 50%.\n14. **\"Method improvement is incremental\"** → Grounded in diagnosis (independence + taxonomy), not ad-hoc.\n15. **\"Weighted SC is simpler\"** → exp-016 planned. Weighted SC doesn't address independence.\n16. **\"Within-model ICL transition contradicts regime theory\"** → exp-050b (SC=57.79%, Δ=-3.52pp): ICL changes trace generation characteristics, not just SC level. Paper must distinguish \"same model, different prompt\" from \"same model, same prompt, different accuracy.\"\n\n## Working Title (D092 V3 — G+R dual method)\n**\"Breaking Symbolic Confirmation Bias: Constraint-Answer Decoupling and Cross-Architecture Verification for LLM Logical Reasoning\"**\n(Alternatives:\n- \"Two Cures for One Disease: Decoupling and Architectural Diversity Against Symbolic Confirmation Bias in LLM Reasoning\"\n- \"The Independence Bottleneck in Symbolic LLM Reasoning: Diagnosis, 25+ Failed Interventions, and Two Independent Solutions\"\n- V2 fallback (R only): \"Breaking the Self-Consistency Ceiling: How Cross-Architecture Verification Overcomes Symbolic Confirmation Bias in LLM Reasoning\"\n- V1 fallback (pure negative): \"Beyond Self-Consistency: How Trace Independence Limits Symbolic Consensus in LLM Reasoning\")\n\nNOTE (Dream D037): Title MUST be scoped to \"self-extracted constraints\" / \"SICA\". Published methods (DyLoS, Aristotle, FoVer) use different approaches.\n\n## Paper Structure (D092 V3 — EMNLP 8pp + appendix)\n\n### Sections\n- §1 Introduction: 25+ same-model interventions all fail → independence bottleneck → two independent cures\n- §2 Related Work: SC, neuro-symbolic, Condorcet/voting, verification\n- §3 Diagnostic Framework: Independence Bottleneck (Eff-K, process-κ), Oracle Gap\n- §4 Symbolic Confirmation Bias: bias ratio 4.97×, overlap 94%, causal chain\n- §5 Method 1: Constraint-Answer Decoupling (G) — core method, detailed\n- §6 Method 2: Cross-Architecture NLI Verification (R) — complementary method\n- §7 Combined Analysis: G+R combo, ablation, cross-dataset if available\n- §8 Discussion + Conclusion\n\n### Main Tables\n- T1: Main Results — models × domains, SC% / SICA% / Δpp / p-value, regime boundary\n- T2: Oracle Control — Gold-FOL+MAX-SAT vs SC vs SICA-self, causal separation\n- T3: Independence Quantification — Fleiss' κ / Eff-K / trace agreement per model\n- T4: Confirmation Bias — bias ratio per condition, overlap rate, per-label breakdown\n- T5: Decoupling Results (G) — SC / SICA / G-Decoupled, Δpp, McNemar, per-label\n- T6: NLI Verification Results (R) — SC / NLI standalone / combo w=1,3 / intersection, Δpp, McNemar\n- T7: 25+ Intervention Summary — method / type / dataset / result / failure mode (compact)\n\n### Main Tables (continued)\n- T6: Method-Level Intervention Results — 3 methods × 2 datasets, SC% / Method% / Δpp, failure mode classification\n- T7: κ Generalization — SC / Debate / Diverse-Prompt κ and Eff-K (from exp-077, pending)\n\n### Main Figures\n- F1: Method Pipeline (SICA + oracle control design)\n- F2: SC Accuracy vs SICA Δ scatter (hero figure, regime boundary)\n- F3: K Sensitivity with diversity metrics (K vs Δ, K vs Eff-K)\n- F4: Extraction error breakdown (stacked bar)\n- F5: Method intervention failure modes (grouped bar: ICG/Contrastive/Debate × FOLIO/LogiQA)\n\n### Appendix\n- K ablation quantification\n- Seed replication analysis\n- QwQ-32B / DeepSeek-R1 negative control\n\n## Claim Risk Notes (D059)\n- **Seed replication**: 0/3 significant after D066 correction (p=0.092, 0.070, 1.0). 2/3 positive trend, 1/3 null. Mean +2.12pp. Frame as \"directional trend, not significant effect\" — fragility IS the finding.\n- **Method claims DEAD (D060)**: All three method improvements failed. Paper is pure analysis. 9 remediation failures are EVIDENCE, not limitation.\n- **Condorcet novelty**: ✅ VERIFIED. arXiv:2409.00094 applies CJT to cross-model LLM ensembles (different angle). No prior work applies Fleiss' κ or Eff-K to within-model SC traces. Our framing is novel.\n- **exp-050b complication**: ICL transition Δ=-3.52pp at SC=57.79% complicates simple \"SC determines SICA.\" Explain via trace generation characteristic change.\n- **Variable naming inconsistency**: 56.4% cross-trace pairs share zero Z3 variables. Key mechanism evidence.\n\n## Required Citations (minimum 30 references)\n\n- **SC & extensions**: Wang et al. 2023 (SC), Chen et al. 2023 (USC), Loo et al. 2025 (SC scaling plateaus), Taubenfeld et al. 2025 (weighted SC), DiVeRSe (Li et al. 2023)\n- **Neuro-symbolic reasoning**: DyLoS (EACL 2026), Aristotle (ACL 2025), FoVer (TACL 2025), LogicAgent (2025), Logic-LM (Pan et al. 2023), LINC (Olausson et al. 2023)\n- **Condorcet/voting theory**: Condorcet 1785, Austen-Smith & Banks 1996, **arXiv:2409.00094 (Examining Independence in Ensemble Sentiment Analysis, Sept 2024)** — closest prior work, applies CJT to cross-model LLM ensembles, finds independence violation. Our novelty: within-model trace independence + Fleiss' κ quantification + Eff-K + connection to symbolic aggregation\n- **Test-time compute**: Snell et al. 2025, DeepSeek-R1, Diversity-Aware Training\n- **Verification & reranking**: ORM (Cobbe et al. 2021), PRM (Lightman et al. 2023), Math-Shepherd\n- **Constraint satisfaction**: SATBench (EMNLP 2025), LLM Meets Constraint Propagation (IJCAI 2025)\n- **Debate & multi-agent**: Liang et al. 2023, Du et al. 2023\n\n## Additional Reviewer Concerns (D070/D074 Methods)\n\n17. **\"Why not use a larger model for constraint generation?\"** → Valid. All methods tested at 7B scale. ICG constraint parsing failures + Debate role-play degradation may be scale-dependent. This is explicitly noted as limitation and future work. The contribution is diagnosing the bottleneck and showing it's not trivially fixable, not claiming no fix exists at any scale.\n18. **\"Only tested one model (Mistral-7B) for methods\"** → Methods are interventions on the extraction pipeline, not the base model. Testing on the same model that showed the clearest Goldilocks signal (Mistral-7B FOLIO) is the strongest test — if methods can't improve the best-case scenario, they won't help elsewhere.\n19. **\"ICG multi-seed shows 1/3 positive\"** → Magnitudes (-2.0, +1.5, -1.0) are within SC baseline noise (3.5pp cross-seed). Mean ≈-0.5pp, std=1.8pp. No statistical significance. Properly framed as \"noise-level, neither improving nor degrading.\"\n20. **\"Debate working in other papers\"** → Multi-agent debate literature uses diverse models or much larger models. Same-model 7B debate is a different regime. Our finding is specifically about same-model self-extraction limitations.\n\n## Claim Scoping Rules\n- n=60 conditions: \"suggestive/consistent with\" NOT \"confirming\"\n- n≥200 conditions: can \"confirm\"\n- Combined N>1,300: acceptable if breakdown clearly stated\n- Method claims: must show statistically significant improvement OR clear directional improvement with analysis\n- \"Independence bottleneck\" framing: systematic quantification + theoretical grounding\n\n## V16 Frontier Model Extension (D135–D139)\n\n### New Claim 11: SCB is Universal — 7B to Frontier (D135)\n\nSymbolic Confirmation Bias persists across ALL tested models, from 7B open-source to frontier closed-source:\n\n| Model | Extraction | SC% | SICA% | Δpp | BR(mean) | BR=∞ rate | Process-κ | Answer-κ |\n|-------|-----------|-----|-------|-----|----------|-----------|-----------|----------|\n| Mistral-7B | self | 55.88 | 59.31 | +3.43 | 4.97 | 30% | 0.69* | 0.46 |\n| LLaMA-8B | self | — | — | — | 2.88 | — | — | — |\n| Qwen-14B | self | 75.49 | 75.74 | +0.25 | 3.93 | — | — | 0.74 |\n| R1-Distill-8B | cross→Mistral | 74.51 | 76.96 | +2.45 | 2.92 | — | 0.248 | 0.69 |\n| gpt-4o | self | 73.91 | 72.05 | -1.86 | 2.94 | 44% | 0.79 | 0.80 |\n| gpt-4.1 | cross→Mistral | 82.84 | 81.37 | -1.47 | 2.70 | 58% | 0.792 | 0.86 |\n| gpt-5.5 | cross→Mistral | 84.31 | 84.80 | +0.49(n.s.) | 3.48 | 87% | 0.563 | 0.96 |\n| o3 | cross→Mistral | 82.65 | 82.65 | 0.00 | 1.66 | 82% | 0.596 | 0.92 |\n| Gemini-2.5-Pro* | cross→Mistral | 87.37 | 86.32 | -1.05 | 2.79 | — | 0.790 | 0.75 |\n\n*Gemini: 95/204 interim. BR=∞ rates high in frontier models (44–87%).\nTable footnote MUST distinguish self vs cross extraction context.\n\n### New Claim 12: Process-κ Gradient Predicts Cross-Model Extraction Effectiveness (D137–D139)\n\nCross-model extraction (Mistral-7B extracts FOL from other models' traces) effectiveness is monotonically predicted by Process-κ:\n\n| Model | Process-κ | Δ(SICA-SC) | BR(mean) |\n|-------|-----------|------------|----------|\n| R1-Distill | 0.248 | +2.45pp ✓ | 2.92 |\n| gpt-5.5 | 0.563 | +0.49pp(n.s.) | 3.48 |\n| o3 | 0.596 | 0.00pp | 1.66 |\n| Gemini-2.5-Pro* | 0.790 | -1.05pp | 2.79 |\n| gpt-4.1 | 0.792 | -1.47pp | 2.70 |\n\n**Spearman ρ(κ→Δ) = -1.0, n=5, one-tailed p=0.0083** (statistically significant).\n\n**Paper language constraints (D138)**:\n- Use \"decreases monotonically\" — NOT \"predictive\" or \"significant correlation\"\n- Dual-variable framing: \"two necessary conditions\" (qualitative mechanism), NOT regression model\n  - Low κ (diverse traces) → cross-model extractor can contribute novel constraints\n  - High BR (strong self-bias) → there IS bias to escape from\n  - Both conditions required: o3 (BR=1.66, near-unbiased) → Δ=0 despite moderate κ=0.596\n- o3 BR=1.66 is observation (\"bimodal error pattern: 82% BR=∞ but low finite-BR\"), NOT sub-finding\n- BR=∞ ordering does NOT strictly follow \"model capability\" (o3 82% < gpt-5.5 87%). <details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>
```

```text
Now I have the full paper framing. Let me analyze the 4 questions:

### Q1: Gemini 95-topic interim risk

Current Gemini data: 95/204, SC=83/95 (87.37%), SICA=82/95 (86.32%), Δ=-1.05pp
Process-κ = 0.790

For the monotonic pattern (ρ=-1.0) to break, Gemini's full-204 Δ needs to exceed o3's Δ (0.00pp), i.e., become positive.


To flip the sign, SICA would need to outperform SC by at least 1 problem across the remaining 109 items. If the remaining problems maintain SC's current 87.4% success rate, that's roughly 95 additional correct answers, meaning SICA would need about 96 correct to achieve positive Δ—a marginal difference of less than 1 percentage point. To actually break the monotonic pattern, SICA would need to outperform SC by at least 2 questions in the remaining 109 problems. Given that the interim results showed SICA performing about 1.05pp worse than SC, achieving this reversal would require a significant distribution shift—possible but unlikely since FOLIO problems aren't ordered by difficulty and the first 95 problems should be representative of the full set.

If the true effect remains stable at -1.05pp across all 204 problems, the final delta would be approximately -1.03pp, nearly identical to the interim result. However, the confidence interval is extremely wide—roughly ±9.8pp—meaning the true effect could plausibly range from -11pp to +10pp, so there's substantial uncertainty around whether SICA is genuinely worse or if this is just noise.

For the monotonic ranking pattern where Gemini needs to outperform o3's 0.00pp, the wide confidence interval makes this possible but unlikely if the first 95 problems are representative. The main risk is whether the problem distribution shifts systematically later on—if problems become harder or easier, the interim estimate could diverge significantly from the full result. I'd estimate roughly a 20-30% chance of a sign flip based on the confidence interval width.

On the 70B front, the AWQ SICA experiment data is stored on a server that's currently unreachable via SSH, though the 70B model refers to Qwen-72B with AWQ quantization and tensor parallelism set to 4.

Looking at what the paper actually claims, 70B doesn't factor into the core narrative. The existing coverage spans 7B through frontier models, and while 70B would fill a gap between 14B and the largest models, the paper already includes QwQ-32B as a mid-scale reference point showing consistent behavior. With that data point plus the frontier results, the missing 70B isn't critical to the paper's argument about universality across scales.

For the cross-SICA experiment, I'm examining how bidirectional extraction between Mistral and Qwen3 on ProofWriter tests whether cross-model constraints reduce spurious correlations compared to same-model extraction. This setup also lets me investigate whether the independence-accuracy trade-off varies across model pairs and whether the quality ladder holds at the lowest levels.

The 2×2 results could cut either way for the narrative: if cross-model extraction shows minimal or negative gains, it reinforces that spurious correlations are fundamentally structural rather than an artifact of single-model biases; if it shows improvement, that suggests cross-model constraints offer a partial workaround. However, the extraction pipeline for Qwen3 same-model conditions has a data integrity issue that might prevent completing this analysis in time.

Looking at the most likely reviewer critiques, the core vulnerability is that my main positive finding—the Mistral-7B Goldilocks effect at +3.43 percentage points—doesn't reach statistical significance (p=0.092), and none of the three random seeds individually show significance.

Beyond that, I'm concerned about several other pressure points: testing interventions only at 7B scale, relying on interim Gemini results that cover less than half the full dataset, building a correlation claim on just five data points for the process-κ gradient, anchoring all cross-model extraction to a single 7B model which could make the findings Mistral-specific rather than general, and describing the dual-variable mechanism qualitatively without formal regression analysis to back up the κ×BR interaction.

There's also the question of why the paper doesn't directly compare against published alternatives like DyLoS, Aristotle, and FoVer instead of just noting they use different approaches. I should focus my response on the most defensible and actionable of these concerns.
```
